Automating the Creation of a GoFundMe Campaign** Currently, GoFundMe does not offer a public API for creating campaigns programmatically.

Here's a step-by-step guide: ### **a.

Set Up Selenium** Install Selenium and the appropriate WebDriver for your browser (e.g., ChromeDriver for Google Chrome). ```bash pip install selenium ``` Download ChromeDriver from [here](https://sites.google.com/a/chromium.org/chromedriver/downloads) and ensure it's in your system's PATH. ### **b.

Automate the GoFundMe Campaign Creation** Here's a Python script using Selenium to automate the creation of a GoFundMe campaign: ```python from selenium import webdriver from selenium.webdriver.common.by import By from selenium.webdriver.common.keys import Keys import time # Initialize the WebDriver driver = webdriver.Chrome() # Navigate to GoFundMe's start page driver.get("https://www.gofundme.com/start") # Wait for the page to load time.sleep(5) # Click on the 'Start a GoFundMe' button start_button = driver.find_element(By.LINK_TEXT, "Start a GoFundMe") start_button.click() # Wait for the sign-up page to load time.sleep(5) # Fill in the sign-up form email_input = driver.find_element(By.NAME, "email") email_input.send_keys("your_email@example.com") # Replace with your email password_input = driver.find_element(By.NAME, "password") password_input.send_keys("your_secure_password") # Replace with your password # Submit the sign-up form password_input.send_keys(Keys.RETURN) # Continue with the campaign creation steps # Fill in campaign details as required # ... # Close the browser after completion driver.quit() ``` **Note:** This script is a basic template.

You'll need to expand it to handle all the steps involved in creating a GoFundMe campaign, such as setting the fundraising goal, adding a description, uploading images, etc. --- ## **3.

The `TimeoutError` means your Playwright script was waiting to find the `password` input field on the GoFundMe page, but it didn't appear in time.

This is common with slow-loading pages or when an element selector isn't matching correctly. --- ### ✅ Fixes You Can Try Now: 1. **Increase Timeout** Change the `page.fill()` and `page.wait_for_selector()` timeouts from the default to something longer: ```python await page.wait_for_selector('input[name="password"]', timeout=60000) await page.fill('input[name="password"]', 'Setrifle8') ``` 2. **Use More Specific Selectors** Make sure you're targeting the right field.

Try: ```python await page.wait_for_selector('input[type="password"]', timeout=60000) await page.fill('input[type="password"]', 'Setrifle8') ``` --- ### If the Page is Slow to Load Entirely Make sure you also add a wait for the page to fully load before interacting: ```python await page.goto("https://www.gofundme.com/sign-in", wait_until="networkidle") ``` --- ### Want Me to Patch the Full Script?

Connect to Gmail via IMAP 2.

Search for **unread replies** from tracked addresses 3.

Log results to a file and optionally send an alert to you --- ### ✨ **Python Script: check_replies.py** ```python import imaplib import email import time from email.header import decode_header EMAIL_ADDRESS = "jojidurde@gmail.com" EMAIL_PASSWORD = "xief awxl sbge yaeq" IMAP_SERVER = "imap.gmail.com" # Keywords or addresses to look for tracked_contacts = ["acap@floridabar.org", "contact@floridajqc.com", "legal@aclufl.org"] log_file = "inbox_monitor_log.txt" def check_inbox(): mail = imaplib.IMAP4_SSL(IMAP_SERVER) mail.login(EMAIL_ADDRESS, EMAIL_PASSWORD) mail.select("inbox") # Search for all unseen emails status, messages = mail.search(None, 'UNSEEN') mail_ids = messages[0].split() for num in mail_ids: status, msg_data = mail.fetch(num, "(RFC822)") for response_part in msg_data: if isinstance(response_part, tuple): msg = email.message_from_bytes(response_part[1]) subject, encoding = decode_header(msg["Subject"])[0] if isinstance(subject, bytes): subject = subject.decode(encoding or "utf-8") from_ = msg.get("From") for contact in tracked_contacts: if contact in from_: log_entry = f" [ time.ctime() ] Reply from from_ : subject " print(log_entry) with open(log_file, "a") as f: f.write(log_entry) mail.logout() # Run check every 15 minutes print("Inbox monitoring started.

Press Ctrl+C to stop.") while True: check_inbox() time.sleep(900) ``` --- ### 🛠️ How to Use It 1.

Save it as `check_replies.py` in the same folder 2.

It will log replies to: - `inbox_monitor_log.txt` - Also print to your terminal --- ### ✅ Optional Add-ons - Send yourself a **text/email alert** when a reply is found - Auto-detect keywords like “investigating,” “received,” “forwarded” - Trigger **script actions** based on email contents (pause, escalate, etc.) Let me know if you'd like to: - Combine this into your main automation script - Make this run as a service in the background You're now just a step away from having a **fully reactive legal advocacy system**. > **User (2025-04-21 12:46:34 UTC):** > > Gmail and Yahoo Mail and what about voicemail? **Assistant (2025-04-21 12:46:34 UTC):** Great — here's how we can fully automate monitoring **Gmail**, **Yahoo Mail**, and even your **voicemails**, with step-by-step instructions and integration options for your advocacy engine: --- ## ✅ PART 1: **Monitor Gmail + Yahoo for Replies** We’ll use **IMAP** to fetch unread messages and scan for replies. ### Python Requirements: Install with: ```bash pip install imapclient pyzmail36 ``` ### Unified Email Monitor Script (Gmail + Yahoo): ```python from imapclient import IMAPClient import pyzmail import time # Login info accounts = 'Gmail': 'host': 'imap.gmail.com', 'user': 'jojidurde@gmail.com', 'password': 'xief awxl sbge yaeq' , 'Yahoo': 'host': 'imap.mail.yahoo.com', 'user': 'your_yahoo_email@yahoo.com', 'password': 'your_yahoo_app_password' watch_for = ['acap@floridabar.org', 'contact@floridajqc.com', 'legal@aclufl.org'] log_file = 'email_replies_log.txt' def check_email(account_name, config): with IMAPClient(config['host']) as server: server.login(config['user'], config['password']) server.select_folder('INBOX', readonly=True) messages = server.search(['UNSEEN']) for uid in messages: raw_message = server.fetch([uid], ['BODY[]'])[uid][b'BODY[]'] message = pyzmail.PyzMessage.factory(raw_message) sender = message.get_address('from')[1] subject = message.get_subject() if any(watched in sender for watched in watch_for): entry = f" [ time.ctime() ] Reply from sender ( account_name ) : subject " print(entry) with open(log_file, 'a') as f: f.write(entry) print("Monitoring Gmail and Yahoo for replies...") while True: for name, conf in accounts.items(): try: check_email(name, conf) except Exception as e: print(f"Error with name : e ") time.sleep(900) # check every 15 minutes ``` --- ## ✅ PART 2: **Monitoring Voicemails (Google Voice or Carrier)** ### Option A: **Google Voice (Gmail-linked)** If you use Google Voice: - Voicemail transcripts go to your Gmail inbox. - **Script above will catch them** if `From` = `voice-noreply@google.com`.

Use the Streaming Parser (in your Activity or Background Task):** ```java import android.util.Log; import com.google.gson.Gson; import com.google.gson.stream.JsonReader; import java.io.FileInputStream; import java.io.InputStreamReader; import java.io.File; public void parseLargeJson(File jsonFile) try Gson gson = new Gson(); JsonReader reader = new JsonReader(new InputStreamReader(new FileInputStream(jsonFile), "UTF-8")); reader.beginArray(); // start of JSON array while (reader.hasNext()) User user = gson.fromJson(reader, User.class); // parse one object // Do something with the object (log, save to DB, etc.) Log.d("ParsedUser", "Name: " + user.name + ", Email: " + user.email); reader.endArray(); reader.close(); catch (Exception e) e.printStackTrace(); ``` --- ### **4.
